# ENERGRID M261 Point Reference

Generated by `codegen/gen_docs.py` from `catalog/point_catalog.json`. DO NOT EDIT.

**1513 points** across 8 devices. Every write-capable point is a `setpoint`; every other point is read-only (`alarm` or `telemetry`) as seen by both protocols. `IEC-104` and `Modbus` are the addresses a client actually uses; `Readback IEC-104` (setpoints only) is the read-only mirror the same value shows up at after a write — see the simulator's own point catalog for the authoritative machine-readable form (`catalog/point_catalog.json`, private/internal).
